    @pytest.mark.error
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "setup,test_id",
        [
            ("nonexistent", "file-not-found"),
            ("invalid-yaml", "invalid-yaml-syntax"),
            ("non-mapping", "non-mapping-yaml"),
        ],
        ids=["file-not-found", "invalid-yaml-syntax", "non-mapping-yaml"],
    )
    def test_create_unparseable_file(
        run_cli, tmp_path: Path, setup: str, test_id: str
    ) -> None:
        """mesh create with missing, unparsable, or non-mapping input yields parse error."""
        if setup == "nonexistent":
            result = run_cli(["mesh", "create", "-f", str(tmp_path / "nope.yaml")])
        elif setup == "invalid-yaml":
            bad_file = tmp_path / "bad.yaml"
            bad_file.write_text(":::invalid yaml{{{\n")
            result = run_cli(["mesh", "create", "-f", str(bad_file)])
        else:  # non-mapping
            scalar_file = tmp_path / "scalar.yaml"
            scalar_file.write_text("just a string\n")
            result = run_cli(["mesh", "create", "-f", str(scalar_file)])
    
>       assert_error_contains(result, field="", error_type="parse")

        ],

    @pytest.mark.functionality
    def test_vault_ready_after_mesh_resume(run_cli) -> None:
        """Vault status transitions to Ready after the referenced mesh becomes stable again."""
        create_mesh(run_cli, "my-mesh", spec={"instances": 2})
        stop_mesh(run_cli, "my-mesh")
        create_vault(run_cli, "my-vault", "my-mesh")
    
        # Resume mesh
        run_cli(
            ["mesh", "update"],
            input_yaml={"metadata": {"name": "my-mesh"}, "spec": {"instances": 2}},
        )
    
        # Describe vault — Ready should now be True

    ) -> None:

    @pytest.mark.functionality
    def test_task_fail_prefix(run_cli) -> None:
        """Task inline command prefixed with FAIL: sets state to Failed with command index detail."""
        create_mesh(run_cli, "my-mesh")
        create_task(
            run_cli, "bad-task", "my-mesh", spec={"inline": "FAIL:disk full"}
        )
    
        result = run_cli(["task", "run", "bad-task"])
        assert_success(result)
        assert result.json["status"]["state"] == "Failed"

E         - command 0 failed: disk full
E         ?         ^
E         + command 1 failed: disk full
E         ?         ^

run_cli = <function run_cli.<locals>._run at 0x7cfdd5229c60>

    @pytest.mark.functionality
    def test_task_partial_failure(run_cli) -> None:
        """Task run reports the exact command index and reason for the first FAIL: line."""
        create_mesh(run_cli, "my-mesh")
        create_task(
            run_cli,

    @pytest.mark.error
    def test_phase_lifecycle_no_reset(run_cli) -> None:

    @pytest.mark.functionality
    def test_regression_migration_and_error_sort(run_cli) -> None:
        """Regression(CP6+CP4): FullStop migration completes; errors sorted by (field, type)."""
        create_mesh(run_cli, "mig-mesh", spec={"runtime": "3.1.1"})
    
        update = run_cli(
            ["mesh", "update"],
            input_yaml={"metadata": {"name": "mig-mesh"}, "spec": {"runtime": "4.0.0"}},
        )
        assert_success(update)
        assert find_condition(update.json, "Migration") is not None
        assert update.json["status"]["stable"] is False
    
        migrate = run_cli(["mesh", "migrate", "mig-mesh"])
        assert_success(migrate)
        assert find_condition(migrate.json, "Migration") is None
        assert migrate.json["status"]["stable"] is True

            input_yaml={
                "metadata": {"name": "BAD"},
